For years we did what you probably do: put money down on DraftKings or FanDuel, take a worse number than we knew we deserved, and hand the house its cut whether we won or lost.</p><p>That always bothered us. The book is not your opponent in the game, it is a middleman taking a tax on your fandom. So we built the thing we wanted to exist: a marketplace where you bet real people, get the open-market price, and keep more of every win. No house on the other side, and no one rooting for you to lose.</p><p>We started this in Minneapolis before Wall Street decided sports were a market. Today 35,000+ bettors are here, and because we do not profit when you lose, a share of what we make goes back through BE 4 Good.
